Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the biological framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 573. The resulting operator achieves bound O(337/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to biological structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 573/337 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on biological structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 671. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 142 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 671 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the biological structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 142. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
